# vLLM issues and PRs for DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash + DSpark (B300 block)

As of 2026-10-20. Scope: every GitHub issue and PR we could find on DeepSeek-V4 / V4.1(-Flash), DSpark and the parts
they run on (sparse MLA / FlashMLA / indexer / top-k, SWA bounded replay, Engram, DeepGEMM MegaMoE, FP8/MXFP4/NVFP4,
DP/EP/DEP, CUDA graphs with spec decode, prefix caching and KV offload, Blackwell kernels, `deepseek_v41`
tokenizer/reasoning/tool parsers), in vllm-project/vllm and the upstream repos that matter. Machine-readable list:
`vllm-dsv41-dspark-items.csv`.

Tags: **vendor** = the PR author's or recipe's own benchmark; **user report** = an issue reporter; **doc** = read in
source code or the PR diff; **estimate** = ours. Nothing here is measured by us.

Base: vLLM v0.32.0 (tag 2026-10-17; branch cut from main 2026-10-12 at `fe341a53`; 16 PRs picked onto the branch after
the cut). "main" = merged on main after the cut and in no release. Release per PR from the git history (`git log <tag>`).

## Findings

| # | Finding | Items | Basis | Action |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Block verification reused random draws across rounds**, so the output distribution was biased in exactly our DSpark mode (probabilistic draft + block verification): P(token) 0.356 vs 0.300 expected; deterministic + block 0.417 vs 0.500. Fixed on main 10-15, MRV2 Python only | [#61161](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61161) (fixes #61114) | vendor: toy Llama target/draft, 10,000 requests | Cherry-pick (low risk, no csrc) |
| 2 | **DSpark output corruption when a prefill-bearing batch is padded into a FULL decode graph** (MRV2 `_is_compatible`). Two open fixes: #62535 (runner: keep prefill batches out of FULL graphs, +8 lines) and #59986 (attention: V4.1 sparse builders report UNIFORM_BATCH). #59970, the same class with adaptive verification, was reproduced on **B300** | [#62417](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/62417), [#62535](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62535), [#59970](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/59970), [#59986](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59986) | user report: 4/7 probes corrupted (SM120, FULL); 4/4 crashes with adaptive on (SM10x) | Cherry-pick #62535; test on block (thinking + tool probes at mixed load) |
| 3 | **V4.1 encoder fixed to the 1017 reference encoding for both checkpoints**: tool results no longer start a new user turn, so reasoning before a tool call is kept. Changes prompt tokens on agentic turns (prefix keys too). DeepSeek changed the launch repo's `encoding/` as well (HF `314113f`) | [#62199](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62199) (Python; Rust #62200) | doc | Cherry-pick #62199 (we run the Python frontend); new golden reference after it |
| 4 | **`persistent_topk` returns indices past the row** (length % 4 ≠ 0, threshold in the `-inf` bin, k < len ≤ 8192). Fix validated on B300 by its author; csrc | [#62466](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/62466), [#62467](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62467) | doc; vendor (sm_103) | Cherry-pick (rebuild) |
| 5 | **New checkpoint DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash-1017** (2026-10-17) is the recipe default: same config, tokenizer and tensor index (510.3 GB); "stronger reasoning, coding, agentic … shorter outputs". We pin the 0909 launch revision `2cba9e42` | recipes [#1136](https://github.com/vllm-project/recipes/pull/1136), vllm #62179 | vendor | User decision before 10-27: a revision is a new run file and a new golden reference |
| 6 | **MegaMoE JIT-compiles on the first request at each new prefill size** unless DeepGEMM warmup covers `fp8_fp4_mega_moe` and `bf16_mega_gate`: 24 of 40 first requests took 3.5–6 s; worst TTFT at c32 8.33 → 1.47 s, on 2×B300 DSv4-Flash. Costs +4–5 min cold start | [#60468](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60468) (main) | vendor (B300) | Cherry-pick (low risk); persist the DeepGEMM JIT cache with the compile cache |
| 7 | **DP hang with 2+ API servers**: an idle or paused rank can block forever in the DP collective. Our DEP files run `api-server-count 2` | [#59559](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59559) (main) | doc | Cherry-pick (med risk) |
| 8 | **KV tier crash**: a client disconnect during an fs-tier load, plus another request on the same prefix, trips an engine-core assert (whole engine down). Also: backpressure drop rate 75% applied as 50%; per-worker CPU pinning; KV transfer results collected before the drafter | [#59096](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59096), #59140, #59544, #62041 (main) | doc | Cherry-pick #59096, #59140, #62041 before any `-kvtier` run; #59544 only if tier startup is slow |
| 9 | **MRV2 spec-decode memory safety**: stale shared block-table rows let padded graph work read another request's KV (#61174; linked crosstalk #61160 on v0.31.0); DSpark graph-padding rows overrun the `dsv4_topk` router (#59166, illegal memory access above 256 seqs, H20); sliding-window entries below the window can inject NaN from reused pages (#60767: DFlash2 acceptance 29 → 41% on 2×B300) | #61174, #59166, #60767 (main) | user report / vendor | Cherry-pick #61174; #59166 only if `deep_gemm_mega_moe` routes small batches through `dsv4_topk` (MegaMoE has its own Mega-Gate above M=16, #56266; reported with `humming` MoE on H20); #60767 med risk, check acceptance at c≥32 first |
| 10 | **Our CUDA graph cap is below the DSpark decode batch.** 128 seqs/rank × (k+1)=6 = 768 tokens > `max-cudagraph-capture-size` 512, so decode steps above ~85 seqs/rank leave the full graph. The recipe's B300 rows capture multiples of 6 up to 8190 | recipe YAML (current) | doc, estimate | Config: a `-g1024` run file with sizes in multiples of 6 (next block) |
| 11 | **NaN in MegaAttn prefill from an unseeded index workspace**: the V4.1 copy of `combine_topk_swa_indices` does not fill the caller buffer with −1, so FlashMLA mega-attention prefill reads stale indices for the first 127 tokens of a prompt, **the rows right after an SWA bounded-replay start**, and short compressed rows; NaN spreads through KV into the indexer (illegal memory access downstream). One line; the same fix (#55299) is in `deepseek_v4` only. (Also: the recipe now turns adaptive verification on for NVIDIA; we keep it off, fix #59986 open) | [#60613](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60613) (open, +1 line) | doc; user report (their deployment) | Cherry-pick; it sits on our backend and on replay-on runs |
| 12 | **SM103-specific numerics: teacher-forced logprobs on V4.1 shifted on GB300 (SM103) after #56893 (MXFP8 KV record stores RoPE dims as FP8)**: agent/tool-transcript top-1 flips 1.77% → 12.66%, held-out math/JP/DE 0% → 9%; whole-corpus perplexity flat; DSpark acceptance on shell prompts 0.908 → 0.829. The reporter also ran a local MoE hook (same on both arms) | [#58391](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/58391) (open) | user report (GB300 = SM103) | Test on block: golden NLL split by class (tool/structured vs prose), not only the mean (ENG-W2) |
| 13 | **B300 rank wedge**: the V4 sparse-decode MLA kernel spins in TMEM alloc with no timeout on sm_103 DEP4; one rank wedges every few hours (100% util at idle power) and the other ranks time out in a DeepGEMM NVLink barrier | [#51035](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/51035) (open since v0.26.0, no fix linked) | user report (8×B300) | Test on block: L7 is the soak; the F3 rank-hang path must catch it |
| 14 | **DSpark and prefix/offload caching interact badly**: the DSpark draft KV group vetoes GPU prefix hits (#48455), makes the OffloadingConnector record 0 external hits for the server lifetime (#47890, still open 10-18), and the EAGLE tail-block drop is untested for ≥3 groups (#51771: 0% vs 72.7% hits); fixes #47891, #48459, #57110, #47926 open. Separately, acceptance falls above 128K context (Kimi-K3 + DSpark on B300: 2.4 → 1.25), not caused by restored KV; opt-in `draft_context_window` #61261 restores 2.2 | #48455, #47890, #51771, #47891, #48459, #57110, #47930, #61261 | user report | Test on block: L3/L4 external hits > 0 with DSpark, ENG-W3 `cached_tokens`, acceptance by context length (B12) |
| 15 | **Tool-call and encoding failures**: DSML tool-call failures tied to prefix-cache reuse on DSv4-Flash (#59926: 41/65 failures on head-only hits; a dedicated `cache_salt` cut them 35/138 → 3/203); DeepSeek's own reference encoder differs from the official API on user-typed special tokens and on kept reasoning (deepseek-recipe #5, #6); reasoning loops in long tool conversations (deepseek-recipe #24). Root causes open | [#59926](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/59926), deepseek-recipe [#5](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-recipe/issues/5), [#6](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-recipe/issues/6), [#24](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-recipe/issues/24) | user report | Test on block: A09 tool-call conformance on cold and cached prefixes; a long tool-conversation replay |

Also corrected or noted (doc): #60029 (64-head FlashMLA prefill) and #59709 (C128 compressor PIECEWISE skip) are not on
our V4.1 MegaAttn path, so `vllm-upgrade-0.32.md` overstates #60029; #61039 (length-aware decode indexer, ITL −3% on
GB300) is the best perf pick after the correctness fixes; InferenceX #3095 sets a CUDA-graph capture floor of 64 on B300
for c1–c4 (ITL p90 38.74 → 2.62 ms, vendor); KV per token with DSpark checkpoints is ~8× the preview's (#51041, #51799,
#52495 open), which L1 should record.

| Source | How | Items |
|---|---|---|

| deepseek-ai repos | REST list of all issues/PRs since 2026-04-01: FlashMLA, DeepGEMM, DeepEP, DeepSelect, DeepSpec, DeepJIT, TileKernels, Engram, deepseek-recipe | 637 |
| GitHub search | 116 queries (list below) over vllm-project/vllm, flashinfer-ai/flashinfer, vllm-project/recipes, SemiAnalysisAI/InferenceX; 8,199 unique hits; kept items created ≥ 2026-04-01 whose title/body/files match the V4 term set | 3816 |

Every item was fetched with body, the first and last 6 comments, cross-references and (PRs) changed files, then read and classified (title alone was never used). **Not classified (1532 items, action `auto` in the CSV):** search hits whose title does not name V4/DSpark or a component and that matched only in the body (mostly env dumps and other models); their group is a keyword guess. Gaps: Hugging Face model-repo discussions, SGLang, Slack/Discord; flashinfer items were kept only when the title matched; DeepEP is listed but not on our MoE path.

## For the fork bitlane/v0.32.0.1

Ranked by what it protects on the block. All are merged on main after the v0.32.0 cut unless marked open. "csrc" =
binary change (needs a wheel/image rebuild, not a Python overlay). Risk: low = small and local; med = touches the
scheduler, model runner or a shared kernel path; high = large or depends on other unpicked PRs. Self-contained: the
parent can merge this with the commit-range view.
